Job `build`: matrix uses explicit `include` entries \u2014 `{os: macos-14, target: aarch64-apple-darwin, arch: arm64}` and `{os: macos-13, target: x86_64-apple-darwin, arch: x86_64}`. Steps: checkout \u2192 setup-python 3.12 \u2192 setup-node 20 + pnpm 9 \u2192 rust toolchain (`dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable` with `targets: ${{ matrix.target }}`) \u2192 `pip install -e \".[dev,build]\"` \u2192 `make build-sidecar ARCH=${{ matrix.arch }}` \u2192 `pnpm -C app install --frozen-lockfile` \u2192 import .p12 cert into a temporary keychain (random password, deleted via `trap` on exit) using `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64` + `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD` \u2192 write .p8 to `~/.appstoreconnect/private_keys/AuthKey_${APPLE_API_KEY_ID}.p8` from `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64` \u2192 `pnpm -C app tauri build --target ${{ matrix.target }}` (the `--target` flag is mandatory) \u2192 upload `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig` artifacts named with `${{ matrix.target }}` for disambiguation. The build step's `env:` block MUST include all of: `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` (the secret name keeps Apple terminology; Tauri reads the env var named APPLE_API_KEY), `APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY_PATH: <path>`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}`. All 9 documented secrets (including `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`) must appear somewhere in the file. Job `publish`: `needs: [build]`, runs on `ubuntu-latest`, downloads both arch artifacts, runs `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` to produce `latest.json`, then uses `gh release create $TAG --generate-notes` (or `gh release upload` if release exists) to publish the `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig`, and `latest.json`.",

The script reads each `.sig` via `cat`, fails fast if either platform's `.sig` is missing (so we never publish a `latest.json` pointing at a nonexistent asset), and emits JSON conforming to tauri-plugin-updater v2's `latest.json` schema: top-level `version`, `pub_date` (ISO 8601 UTC), `notes` (optional), and `platforms` containing `darwin-aarch64` and `darwin-x86_64`, each with `signature` (.sig file contents) and `url` (https://github.com/banodoco/dataclaw/releases/download/${VERSION}/<asset>).   "meta_commentary": "EXECUTION GUIDANCE:\n\n1. **Read the existing surface first.** Before T3, open and skim: app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json (current bundle/externalBin shape), app/src-tauri/Cargo.toml (existing plugin deps), app/src-tauri/src/main.rs (current plugin registrations + invoke_handler list), app/src-tauri/capabilities/default.json (existing permissions), app/src-tauri/tests/capabilities.rs (the test you must not break), app/package.json, app/src/App.tsx + main.tsx (where to mount UpdateChecker), Makefile (existing build-app target), .github/workflows/test.yml lines 22-60. This 5-minute read prevents the 80% of avoidable mistakes.\n\n2. **Keypair generation is a one-shot manual step in T3.** Run `pnpm -C app tauri signer generate -w ~/.tauri/dataclaw.key` outside the repo. Capture stdout: it will print `Public key:` followed by a base64 string. Paste THAT into tauri.conf.json `plugins.updater.pubkey`. Stash the private key file at `~/.tauri/dataclaw.key` and the [REDACTED] the maintainer (not git) can retrieve them \u2014 both will become GitHub Secrets `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` (file contents) and `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD`. If the secret is later rotated, the public key in tauri.conf.json must be updated and a transition release signed with both old + new keys (documented in RELEASING.md per T1).\n\n3. **Atomic compile group: T4 \u2192 T5 \u2192 T6.** Adding `tauri-plugin-updater` to Cargo.toml without registering the plugin compiles fine (Rust ignores unused deps), but registering the plugin without the dep won't. Add the dep first, then write updater.rs + main.rs edits, then capability. The compile won't pass cleanly until T4+T5+T6 are all done; sequence them in one branch.\n\n4. **The signingIdentity shell-substitution caveat.** `\"signingIdentity\": \"${APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY-}\"` relies on Tauri's bundler expanding the variable from process env at build time AND treating empty/null as \"skip codesign\". This is the Tauri 2 documented behavior, but verify with `make release-local` after T3 (validation step (4) of T14). If the substitution fails to expand or the empty string fails as a literal identity, the documented fallback is a Makefile-level JSON patch \u2014 note the deviation in executor_notes for T3 if you need it.\n\n5. **release.yml secret-name \u2192 env-var mapping is the most-flagged risk.** The GitHub Secret stays named `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` (Apple's terminology). The env block remaps it: `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` because Tauri's notarization code reads `APPLE_API_KEY`. The test `test_release_yml_maps_api_key_id_to_apple_api_key` should regex-match `APPLE_API_KEY:\\s*\\$\\{\\{\\s*secrets\\.APPLE_API_KEY_ID\\s*\\}\\}` (or equivalent) across lines, since YAML may break across two lines.\n\n6. **The `--target` flag is mandatory.** Without `tauri build --target aarch64-apple-darwin` (and the matching x86_64 invocation), GitHub Actions runner labels alone cannot guarantee architecture separation, and the matrix can ship two arm64 binaries with the same arch in `latest.json`.\n\n7. **Do not run actual `pnpm tauri build` on Linux/CI in T14.** Validation step (4) (`make release-local`) only works on macOS with Xcode toolchain. If executing in a non-macOS environment or without the Tauri toolchain, document the skip in T14 executor_notes with the specific reason (\"no macOS host available\"), and verify static config + run pytest + cargo test as a fallback. Do not attempt to fake the build success.\n\n8. **Avoid these debt-watch traps:** don't make tauri.conf.json `pubkey` an env substitution (debt: `tauri-config`); don't add new dependencies for tests that can be done with stdlib (debt avoidance pattern); don't break existing capabilities tests (debt: not regressing existing tests).\n\n9. **Repo slug for the updater endpoint URL** is `banodoco/dataclaw` per `pyproject.toml` Homepage/Repository. Verify before pasting into tauri.conf.json.\n\n10. **Manual `info` criteria (notarization spctl check, end-to-end updater install) cannot be automated in T14.** Surface them clearly in the PR description so the maintainer can run them after pushing v0.4.0-rc.1 to a test fork.",

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked `runpodconfig.from_env()` in `../runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/config.py`. it parses `runpod_ssh_private_key`, `runpod_ssh_private_key_path`, and related key vars, but it does not consume `runpod_ssh_password`; the current password path comes from pod ssh details inside `_build_ssh_client()`. the fallback clause therefore misstates the existing config surface when it says it reads the same env vars that `runpodconfig.from_env()` already consumes. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

If baseline_test_failures is null, the baseline could not be captured; use your judgment but err on the side of assuming failures are regressions. You MUST still re-run the FULL test suite with your changes applied — pre-existing failures do not excuse skipping verification. Never narrow to individual test functions and stop.
        - If this batch includes the final verification task, write a short script that reproduces the exact bug described in the task, run it to confirm the fix resolves it, then delete the script.

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/dataclaw/docs/RELEASING.md\n+# Releasing DataClaw\n+\n+This runbook covers the manual Apple setup, GitHub Secrets, updater signing keys, tagged release flow, and post-release checks for signed and notarized macOS releases.\n+\n+## 1. Enroll in Apple Developer Program\n+\n+1. Join the Apple Developer Program for the account or organization that owns the release.\n+2. In Apple Developer, create a `Developer ID Application` certificate.\n+3. Install the certificate in Keychain Access on the Mac that will export it.\n+4. Export the certificate and private key as a [REDACTED] `.p12` file.\n+\n+```sh\n+# Keychain Access UI:\n+# My Certificates -> Developer ID Application: <Team Name> -> Export...\n+# Save as cert.p12 and set a strong export password.\n+```\n+\n+5. Record the exact signing identity string shown by `security find-identity`.\n+\n+```sh\n+security find-identity -v -p codesigning\n+```\n+\n+Use the full `Developer ID Application: ... (TEAMID)` value as the GitHub Secret `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY`.\n+\n+## 2. Create App Store Connect API key\n+\n+1. Open App Store Connect.\n+2. Go to `Users and Access` -> `Integrations` -> `App Store Connect API`.\n+3. Create an API key with access that can notarize Developer ID applications.\n+4. Download the `.p8` key file once. Apple will not let you download it again.\n+5. Record:\n+   - Key ID -> GitHub Secret `APPLE_API_KEY_ID`\n+   - Issuer ID -> GitHub Secret `APPLE_API_ISSUER`\n+   - Team ID -> GitHub Secret `APPLE_TEAM_ID`\n+\n+## 3. Generate the updater ed25519 keypair\n+\n+Generate the Tauri updater signing key outside the repository.\n+\n+```sh\n+mkdir -p ~/.tauri\n+pnpm -C app tauri signer generate -w ~/.tauri/dataclaw.key\n+```\n+\n+The command prints a public key and writes the private key file to `~/.tauri/dataclaw.key`.\n+\n+1. Copy the printed `Public key:` value into `app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json` at `plugins.updater.pubkey`.\n+2. Commit the public key in `tauri.conf.json`.\n+3. Never commit `~/.tauri/dataclaw.key`.\n+4. Store the private key file contents as GitHub Secret `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY`.\n+5. Store the private key password printed by the command as GitHub Secret `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD`.\n+\n+```sh\n+pbcopy < ~/.tauri/dataclaw.key\n+# Paste into GitHub [REDACTED]\n+\n+# Copy the password printed by `tauri signer generate`.\n+# Paste it into GitHub [REDACTED]\n+```\n+\n+## 4. Base64 encode Apple secrets\n+\n+GitHub Actions receives the certificate and API key as base64-encoded strings.\n+\n+```sh\n+base64 -i cert.p12 -o cert.p12.b64\n+base64 -i AuthKey_ABC123DEFG.p8 -o AuthKey_ABC123DEFG.p8.b64\n+\n+pbcopy < cert.p12.b64\n+# Paste into GitHub [REDACTED]\n+\n+pbcopy < AuthKey_ABC123DEFG.p8.b64\n+# Paste into GitHub [REDACTED]\n+```\n+\n+## 5. Configure GitHub Secrets\n+\n+Create these repository secrets before pushing a release tag.\n+\n+| GitHub Secret | Used as | Description |\n+| --- | --- | --- |\n+| `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64` | Certificate import input | Base64 contents of the exported Developer ID Application `.p12`. |\n+| `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD` | Certificate import input | Password used when exporting the `.p12`. |\n+| `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64` | Notarization API key file input | Base64 contents of the App Store Connect `.p8` key. |\n+| `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` | Tauri env var `APPLE_API_KEY` | Apple Key ID. The secret keeps Apple's name, but `release.yml` must rename it to env var `APPLE_API_KEY` because Tauri reads `APPLE_API_KEY`. |\n+| `APPLE_API_ISSUER` | Tauri env var `APPLE_API_ISSUER` | App Store Connect Issuer ID. |\n+| `APPLE_TEAM_ID` | Tauri env var `APPLE_TEAM_ID` | Apple Developer Team ID. |\n+| `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY` | Tauri env var `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY` | Full `Developer ID Application: ... (TEAMID)` signing identity. |\n+| `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` | Tauri updater signing key | Contents of `~/.tauri/dataclaw.key`. Never commit this. |\n+| `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD` | Tauri updater signing password | Password printed by `pnpm -C app tauri signer generate`. Never commit this. |\n+\n+## 6. Cut a signed release\n+\n+1. Confirm local unsigned builds still work before tagging.\n+\n+```sh\n+APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY=\"\" pnpm -C app tauri build\n+```\n+\n+2. Create and push a release tag.\n+\n+```sh\n+git tag v0.4.0\n+git push --tags\n+```\n+\n+3. GitHub Actions runs `.github/workflows/release.yml`.\n+4. The release workflow builds on `macos-14` for `aarch64-apple-darwin` and `macos-13` for `x86_64-apple-darwin`.\n+5. Signing and notarization happen only for tagged releases. Pull requests run unsigned builds.\n+6. The workflow uploads the `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig`, and signed `latest.json` release assets.\n+\n+## 7. Verify the release manually\n+\n+1. Download the released `.dmg` from GitHub Releases.\n+2. Install `DataClaw.app` into `/Applications`.\n+3. Verify Gatekeeper accepts the app as notarized.\n+\n+```sh\n+spctl --assess --type execute /Applications/DataClaw.app\n+```\n+\n+Expected output:\n+\n+```text\n+/Applications/DataClaw.app: accepted\n+source=Notarized Developer ID\n+```\n+\n+4. Launch the app and check for an update from a previously installed signed version before announcing the release.\n+\n+## 8. Rotate the updater signing key\n+\n+Use this when the updater private key or password may have been exposed, or when rotating credentials on a fixed schedule.\n+\n+1. Preserve the old updater private key and password until the cutover release has reached users. Existing installed apps can only verify updates signed by the old public key.\n+2. Generate a new keypair outside the repository.\n+\n+```sh\n+pnpm -C app tauri signer generate -w ~/.tauri/dataclaw-rotated.key\n+```\n+\n+3. Replace `plugins.updater.pubkey` in `app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json` with the new printed public key and commit it.\n+4. Update GitHub Secrets for future releases:\n+\n+```sh\n+pbcopy < ~/.tauri/dataclaw-rotated.key\n+# Paste into GitHub [REDACTED]\n+\n+# Paste the new printed password into GitHub [REDACTED]\n+```\n+\n+5. Sign the cutover release with both old and new updater keys:\n+   - Publish the normal `latest.json` signed with the old key so currently installed apps can install the cutover build.\n+   - Also produce and retain a new-key-signed updater manifest for verification against the committed new public key.\n+6. After the cutover build is installed, those apps contain the new public key and can verify future releases signed only by the new key.\n+7. Remove the old key from operational use only after the cutover window is complete.\n+\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/dataclaw/app/src-tauri/entitlements.plist\n+<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"UTF-8\"?>\n+<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC \"-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN\" \"http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd\">\n+<plist version=\"1.0\">\n+<dict>\n+\t<key>com.apple.security.cs.allow-jit</key>\n+\t<false/>\n+\t<key>com.apple.security.cs.allow-unsigned-executable-memory</key>\n+\t<false/>\n+\t<!-- Flip disable-library-validation to true only if the bundled PyInstaller sidecar fails to load shared libs at runtime; document the failing library and loader error before changing it. -->\n+\t<key>com.apple.security.cs.disable-library-validation</key>\n+\t<false/>\n+\t<key>com.apple.security.cs.allow-dyld-environment-variables</key>\n+\t<false/>\n+</dict>\n+</plist>\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Job `build`: matrix uses explicit `include` entries \u2014 `{os: macos-14, target: aarch64-apple-darwin, arch: arm64}` and `{os: macos-13, target: x86_64-apple-darwin, arch: x86_64}`. Steps: checkout \u2192 setup-python 3.12 \u2192 setup-node 20 + pnpm 9 \u2192 rust toolchain (`dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable` with `targets: ${{ matrix.target }}`) \u2192 `pip install -e \".[dev,build]\"` \u2192 `make build-sidecar ARCH=${{ matrix.arch }}` \u2192 `pnpm -C app install --frozen-lockfile` \u2192 import .p12 cert into a temporary keychain (random password, deleted via `trap` on exit) using `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64` + `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD` \u2192 write .p8 to `~/.appstoreconnect/private_keys/AuthKey_${APPLE_API_KEY_ID}.p8` from `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64` \u2192 `pnpm -C app tauri build --target ${{ matrix.target }}` (the `--target` flag is mandatory) \u2192 upload `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig` artifacts named with `${{ matrix.target }}` for disambiguation. The build step's `env:` block MUST include all of: `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` (the secret name keeps Apple terminology; Tauri reads the env var named APPLE_API_KEY), `APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY_PATH: <path>`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}`. All 9 documented secrets (including `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`) must appear somewhere in the file. Job `publish`: `needs: [build]`, runs on `ubuntu-latest`, downloads both arch artifacts, runs `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` to produce `latest.json`, then uses `gh release create $TAG --generate-notes` (or `gh release upload` if release exists) to publish the `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig`, and `latest.json`.",

The script reads each `.sig` via `cat`, fails fast if either platform's `.sig` is missing (so we never publish a `latest.json` pointing at a nonexistent asset), and emits JSON conforming to tauri-plugin-updater v2's `latest.json` schema: top-level `version`, `pub_date` (ISO 8601 UTC), `notes` (optional), and `platforms` containing `darwin-aarch64` and `darwin-x86_64`, each with `signature` (.sig file contents) and `url` (https://github.com/banodoco/dataclaw/releases/download/${VERSION}/<asset>).   "meta_commentary": "EXECUTION GUIDANCE:\n\n1. **Read the existing surface first.** Before T3, open and skim: app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json (current bundle/externalBin shape), app/src-tauri/Cargo.toml (existing plugin deps), app/src-tauri/src/main.rs (current plugin registrations + invoke_handler list), app/src-tauri/capabilities/default.json (existing permissions), app/src-tauri/tests/capabilities.rs (the test you must not break), app/package.json, app/src/App.tsx + main.tsx (where to mount UpdateChecker), Makefile (existing build-app target), .github/workflows/test.yml lines 22-60. This 5-minute read prevents the 80% of avoidable mistakes.\n\n2. **Keypair generation is a one-shot manual step in T3.** Run `pnpm -C app tauri signer generate -w ~/.tauri/dataclaw.key` outside the repo. Capture stdout: it will print `Public key:` followed by a base64 string. Paste THAT into tauri.conf.json `plugins.updater.pubkey`. Stash the private key file at `~/.tauri/dataclaw.key` and the [REDACTED] the maintainer (not git) can retrieve them \u2014 both will become GitHub Secrets `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` (file contents) and `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD`. If the secret is later rotated, the public key in tauri.conf.json must be updated and a transition release signed with both old + new keys (documented in RELEASING.md per T1).\n\n3. **Atomic compile group: T4 \u2192 T5 \u2192 T6.** Adding `tauri-plugin-updater` to Cargo.toml without registering the plugin compiles fine (Rust ignores unused deps), but registering the plugin without the dep won't. Add the dep first, then write updater.rs + main.rs edits, then capability. The compile won't pass cleanly until T4+T5+T6 are all done; sequence them in one branch.\n\n4. **The signingIdentity shell-substitution caveat.** `\"signingIdentity\": \"${APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY-}\"` relies on Tauri's bundler expanding the variable from process env at build time AND treating empty/null as \"skip codesign\". This is the Tauri 2 documented behavior, but verify with `make release-local` after T3 (validation step (4) of T14). If the substitution fails to expand or the empty string fails as a literal identity, the documented fallback is a Makefile-level JSON patch \u2014 note the deviation in executor_notes for T3 if you need it.\n\n5. **release.yml secret-name \u2192 env-var mapping is the most-flagged risk.** The GitHub Secret stays named `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` (Apple's terminology). The env block remaps it: `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` because Tauri's notarization code reads `APPLE_API_KEY`. The test `test_release_yml_maps_api_key_id_to_apple_api_key` should regex-match `APPLE_API_KEY:\\s*\\$\\{\\{\\s*secrets\\.APPLE_API_KEY_ID\\s*\\}\\}` (or equivalent) across lines, since YAML may break across two lines.\n\n6. **The `--target` flag is mandatory.** Without `tauri build --target aarch64-apple-darwin` (and the matching x86_64 invocation), GitHub Actions runner labels alone cannot guarantee architecture separation, and the matrix can ship two arm64 binaries with the same arch in `latest.json`.\n\n7. **Do not run actual `pnpm tauri build` on Linux/CI in T14.** Validation step (4) (`make release-local`) only works on macOS with Xcode toolchain. If executing in a non-macOS environment or without the Tauri toolchain, document the skip in T14 executor_notes with the specific reason (\"no macOS host available\"), and verify static config + run pytest + cargo test as a fallback. Do not attempt to fake the build success.\n\n8. **Avoid these debt-watch traps:** don't make tauri.conf.json `pubkey` an env substitution (debt: `tauri-config`); don't add new dependencies for tests that can be done with stdlib (debt avoidance pattern); don't break existing capabilities tests (debt: not regressing existing tests).\n\n9. **Repo slug for the updater endpoint URL** is `banodoco/dataclaw` per `pyproject.toml` Homepage/Repository. Verify before pasting into tauri.conf.json.\n\n10. **Manual `info` criteria (notarization spctl check, end-to-end updater install) cannot be automated in T14.** Surface them clearly in the PR description so the maintainer can run them after pushing v0.4.0-rc.1 to a test fork.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: inspected `../runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/pod.py` and `../runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/ssh.py`. the existing `_build_ssh_client()` helper returns the package's own `runpod_lifecycle.ssh.sshclient` wrapper, not a raw `paramiko.sshclient`. that means step 0's promise that `pod.open_ssh_client() -> paramiko.sshclient` is a 3-line public wrapper around existing logic is not technically accurate, and step 20's `scp.scpclient(ssh.get_transport())` call would not work against the current wrapper type as written. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked `runpodconfig.from_env()` in `../runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/config.py`. it parses `runpod_ssh_private_key`, `runpod_ssh_private_key_path`, and related key vars, but it does not consume `runpod_ssh_password`; the current password path comes from pod ssh details inside `_build_ssh_client()`. the fallback clause therefore misstates the existing config surface when it says it reads the same env vars that `runpodconfig.from_env()` already consumes.       "id": "T9",
      "description": "Create `.github/workflows/release.yml` triggered on `push: tags: ['v*']` with two jobs. Job `build`: matrix uses explicit `include` entries \u2014 `{os: macos-14, target: aarch64-apple-darwin, arch: arm64}` and `{os: macos-13, target: x86_64-apple-darwin, arch: x86_64}`. Steps: checkout \u2192 setup-python 3.12 \u2192 setup-node 20 + pnpm 9 \u2192 rust toolchain (`dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable` with `targets: ${{ matrix.target }}`) \u2192 `pip install -e \".[dev,build]\"` \u2192 `make build-sidecar ARCH=${{ matrix.arch }}` \u2192 `pnpm -C app install --frozen-lockfile` \u2192 import .p12 cert into a temporary keychain (random password, deleted via `trap` on exit) using `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64` + `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD` \u2192 write .p8 to `~/.appstoreconnect/private_keys/AuthKey_${APPLE_API_KEY_ID}.p8` from `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64` \u2192 `pnpm -C app tauri build --target ${{ matrix.target }}` (the `--target` flag is mandatory) \u2192 upload `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig` artifacts named with `${{ matrix.target }}` for disambiguation. The build step's `env:` block MUST include all of: `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` (the secret name keeps Apple terminology; Tauri reads the env var named APPLE_API_KEY), `APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY_PATH: <path>`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}`. All 9 documented secrets (including `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`) must appear somewhere in the file. Job `publish`: `needs: [build]`, runs on `ubuntu-latest`, downloads both arch artifacts, runs `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` to produce `latest.json`, then uses `gh release create $TAG --generate-notes` (or `gh release upload` if release exists) to publish the `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig`, and `latest.json`.",

      "id": "T10",
      "description": "Create `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` (executable, 0755) starting with `set -euo pipefail`. Inputs via env: `VERSION`, `RELEASE_TAG`, and paths to per-arch `.app.tar.gz` + `.sig` files (e.g. `ARM64_TARBALL`, `ARM64_SIG`, `X86_TARBALL`, `X86_SIG`). The script reads each `.sig` via `cat`, fails fast if either platform's `.sig` is missing (so we never publish a `latest.json` pointing at a nonexistent asset), and emits JSON conforming to tauri-plugin-updater v2's `latest.json` schema: top-level `version`, `pub_date` (ISO 8601 UTC), `notes` (optional), and `platforms` containing `darwin-aarch64` and `darwin-x86_64`, each with `signature` (.sig file contents) and `url` (https://github.com/banodoco/dataclaw/releases/download/${VERSION}/<asset>).   "meta_commentary": "EXECUTION GUIDANCE:\n\n1. **Read the existing surface first.** Before T3, open and skim: app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json (current bundle/externalBin shape), app/src-tauri/Cargo.toml (existing plugin deps), app/src-tauri/src/main.rs (current plugin registrations + invoke_handler list), app/src-tauri/capabilities/default.json (existing permissions), app/src-tauri/tests/capabilities.rs (the test you must not break), app/package.json, app/src/App.tsx + main.tsx (where to mount UpdateChecker), Makefile (existing build-app target), .github/workflows/test.yml lines 22-60. This 5-minute read prevents the 80% of avoidable mistakes.\n\n2. **Keypair generation is a one-shot manual step in T3.** Run `pnpm -C app tauri signer generate -w ~/.tauri/dataclaw.key` outside the repo. Capture stdout: it will print `Public key:` followed by a base64 string. Paste THAT into tauri.conf.json `plugins.updater.pubkey`. Stash the private key file at `~/.tauri/dataclaw.key` and the [REDACTED] the maintainer (not git) can retrieve them \u2014 both will become GitHub Secrets `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` (file contents) and `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD`. If the secret is later rotated, the public key in tauri.conf.json must be updated and a transition release signed with both old + new keys (documented in RELEASING.md per T1).\n\n3. **Atomic compile group: T4 \u2192 T5 \u2192 T6.** Adding `tauri-plugin-updater` to Cargo.toml without registering the plugin compiles fine (Rust ignores unused deps), but registering the plugin without the dep won't. Add the dep first, then write updater.rs + main.rs edits, then capability. The compile won't pass cleanly until T4+T5+T6 are all done; sequence them in one branch.\n\n4. **The signingIdentity shell-substitution caveat.** `\"signingIdentity\": \"${APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY-}\"` relies on Tauri's bundler expanding the variable from process env at build time AND treating empty/null as \"skip codesign\". This is the Tauri 2 documented behavior, but verify with `make release-local` after T3 (validation step (4) of T14). If the substitution fails to expand or the empty string fails as a literal identity, the documented fallback is a Makefile-level JSON patch \u2014 note the deviation in executor_notes for T3 if you need it.\n\n5. **release.yml secret-name \u2192 env-var mapping is the most-flagged risk.** The GitHub Secret stays named `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` (Apple's terminology). The env block remaps it: `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` because Tauri's notarization code reads `APPLE_API_KEY`. The test `test_release_yml_maps_api_key_id_to_apple_api_key` should regex-match `APPLE_API_KEY:\\s*\\$\\{\\{\\s*secrets\\.APPLE_API_KEY_ID\\s*\\}\\}` (or equivalent) across lines, since YAML may break across two lines.\n\n6. **The `--target` flag is mandatory.** Without `tauri build --target aarch64-apple-darwin` (and the matching x86_64 invocation), GitHub Actions runner labels alone cannot guarantee architecture separation, and the matrix can ship two arm64 binaries with the same arch in `latest.json`.\n\n7. **Do not run actual `pnpm tauri build` on Linux/CI in T14.** Validation step (4) (`make release-local`) only works on macOS with Xcode toolchain. If executing in a non-macOS environment or without the Tauri toolchain, document the skip in T14 executor_notes with the specific reason (\"no macOS host available\"), and verify static config + run pytest + cargo test as a fallback. Do not attempt to fake the build success.\n\n8. **Avoid these debt-watch traps:** don't make tauri.conf.json `pubkey` an env substitution (debt: `tauri-config`); don't add new dependencies for tests that can be done with stdlib (debt avoidance pattern); don't break existing capabilities tests (debt: not regressing existing tests).\n\n9. **Repo slug for the updater endpoint URL** is `banodoco/dataclaw` per `pyproject.toml` Homepage/Repository. Verify before pasting into tauri.conf.json.\n\n10. **Manual `info` criteria (notarization spctl check, end-to-end updater install) cannot be automated in T14.** Surface them clearly in the PR description so the maintainer can run them after pushing v0.4.0-rc.1 to a test fork.",

run: pip install -e \".[dev,build]\"\n+\n+      - run: make build-sidecar ARCH=${{ matrix.arch }}\n+\n+      - run: pnpm -C app install --frozen-lockfile\n+\n+      - name: Import signing assets and build\n+        env:\n+          APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64: ${{ secrets.APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64 }}\n+          APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD }}\n+          APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64 }}\n+          APPLE_API_KEY_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}\n+          APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}\n+          APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}\n+          APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}\n+          APPLE_API_KEY_PATH: ~/.appstoreconnect/private_keys/AuthKey_${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}.p8\n+          APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}\n+          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}\n+          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}\n+        run: |\n+          set -euo pipefail\n+\n+          KEYCHAIN_PATH=\"$RUNNER_TEMP/dataclaw-signing.keychain-db\"\n+          KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD=\"$(openssl rand -base64 32)\"\n+          CERT_PATH=\"$RUNNER_TEMP/developer-id-cert.p12\"\n+          cleanup() {\n+            security delete-keychain \"$KEYCHAIN_PATH\" || true\n+          }\n+          trap cleanup EXIT\n+\n+          python - <<'PY'\n+          import base64\n+          import os\n+          from pathlib import Path\n+\n+          Path(os.environ[\"CERT_PATH\"]).write_bytes(\n+              base64.b64decode(os.environ[\"APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64\"])\n+          )\n+          apple_key_path = Path(os.path.expanduser(os.environ[\"APPLE_API_KEY_PATH\"]))\n+          apple_key_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+          apple_key_path.write_bytes(\n+              base64.b64decode(os.environ[\"APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64\"])\n+          )\n+          PY\n+\n+          security create-keychain -p \"$KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD\" \"$KEYCHAIN_PATH\"\n+          security set-keychain-settings -lut 21600 \"$KEYCHAIN_PATH\"\n+          security unlock-keychain -p \"$KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD\" \"$KEYCHAIN_PATH\"\n+          security import \"$CERT_PATH\" -k \"$KEYCHAIN_PATH\" -P \"$APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD\" -T /usr/bin/codesign -T /usr/bin/productbuild\n+          security list-keychains -d user -s \"$KEYCHAIN_PATH\" $(security list-keychains -d user | sed 's/\"//g')\n+          security set-key-partition-list -S apple-tool:,apple:,codesign: -s -k \"$KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD\" \"$KEYCHAIN_PATH\"\n+\n+          pnpm -C app tauri build --target ${{ matrix.target }}\n+\n+      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4\n+        with:\n+          name: dataclaw-${{ matrix.target }}\n+          if-no-files-found: error\n+          path: |\n+            app/src-tauri/target/${{ matrix.target }}/release/bundle/dmg/*.dmg\n+            app/src-tauri/target/${{ matrix.target }}/release/bundle/macos/*.app.tar.gz\n+            app/src-tauri/target/${{ matrix.target }}/release/bundle/macos/*.app.tar.gz.sig\n+\n+  publish:\n+    name: Publish release\n+    needs: [build]\n+    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n+    permissions:\n+      contents: write\n+\n+    steps:\n+      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n+\n+      - uses: actions/download-artifact@v4\n+        with:\n+          path: artifacts\n+\n+      - name: Build latest.json\n+        env:\n+          RELEASE_TAG: ${{ github.ref_name }}\n+          VERSION: ${{ github.ref_name }}\n+        run: |\n+          set -euo pipefail\n+          ARM64_TARBALL=\"$(find artifacts/dataclaw-aarch64-apple-darwin -name '*.app.tar.gz' -print -quit)\"\n+          ARM64_SIG=\"$(find artifacts/dataclaw-aarch64-apple-darwin -name '*.app.tar.gz.sig' -print -quit)\"\n+          X86_TARBALL=\"$(find artifacts/dataclaw-x86_64-apple-darwin -name '*.app.tar.gz' -print -quit)\"\n+          X86_SIG=\"$(find artifacts/dataclaw-x86_64-apple-darwin -name '*.app.tar.gz.sig' -print -quit)\"\n+          export ARM64_TARBALL ARM64_SIG X86_TARBALL X86_SIG\n+          scripts/build-latest-json.sh > latest.json\n+\n+      - name: Publish GitHub release\n+        env:\n+          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}\n+          TAG: ${{ github.ref_name }}\n+        run: |\n+          set -euo pipefail\n+          mapfile -t ASSETS < <(find artifacts -type f \\( -name '*.dmg' -o -name '*.app.tar.gz' -o -name '*.app.tar.gz.sig' \\) | sort)\n+          ASSETS+=(latest.json)\n+          if gh release view \"$TAG\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n+            gh release upload \"$TAG\" \"${ASSETS[@]}\" --clobber\n+          else\n+            gh release create \"$TAG\" \"${ASSETS[@]}\" --generate-notes\n+          fi\n*** End Patch\n"
}

  "watch_items": [
    "FLAG-001 (addressed): tauri.conf.json `plugins.updater.pubkey` MUST hold the literal ed25519 public key emitted by `pnpm tauri signer generate` \u2014 never an empty string and never an env-substitution placeholder. Test `test_tauri_conf_has_updater_plugin` enforces non-empty.",
    "FLAG-002 (addressed): `bundle.createUpdaterArtifacts: true` is required so the Tauri build emits the `.app.tar.gz` + `.sig` pair the updater verifies against. Without this, release.yml uploads non-existent files.",
    "FLAG-003 (addressed): GitHub Secret name is `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` (matching Apple's terminology) but Tauri 2 reads the env var named `APPLE_API_KEY`. The release.yml env block MUST map them: `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}`.",
    "FLAG-004 (addressed): macos-14 vs macos-13 runner labels alone don't guarantee arm64 vs x86_64 builds. The release matrix MUST use explicit `include` entries pairing each OS to its `target` triple, AND `tauri build --target ${{ matrix.target }}` must be passed explicitly to prevent duplicate-arch artifacts.",
    "FLAG-005 (addressed): The Rust updater commands need a UI invocation path. The `UpdateChecker` React component must be mounted on a reachable view, and must gracefully handle 'pubkey'/'signature' errors so unsigned dev builds don't crash.",
    "Hard back-compat constraint: `make release-local` (= `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY=\"\" pnpm -C app tauri build`) MUST still produce an unsigned `.app` after T3. The `\"signingIdentity\": \"${APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY-}\"` shell-substitution in tauri.conf.json relies on Tauri's bundler treating empty as 'skip codesign'. If empirical testing disproves this, fall back to a Makefile-level JSON patch \u2014 note the deviation in executor_notes.",
    "The existing CI matrix at `.github/workflows/test.yml:26` `[macos-14, macos-13]` with `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: \"\"` (line 48-50) MUST stay green. T11 is the explicit checkpoint.",
    "Private-key / password leak risk: NEVER commit the ed25519 private key file or its password to the repo, screenshots, or transcripts. Only the public key goes into tauri.conf.json. The maintainer adds the private materials to GitHub Secrets out-of-band.",
    "All 9 GitHub Secrets must be referenced in release.yml: `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`, `APPLE_API_KEY_ID`, `APPLE_API_ISSUER`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID`, `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD`.     "Entitlements default: `com.apple.security.cs.disable-library-validation = false`. Only flip to `true` if the bundled PyInstaller sidecar fails to load shared libs \u2014 and document the why with an inline comment.",

  "meta_commentary": "EXECUTION GUIDANCE:\n\n1. **Read the existing surface first.** Before T3, open and skim: app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json (current bundle/externalBin shape), app/src-tauri/Cargo.toml (existing plugin deps), app/src-tauri/src/main.rs (current plugin registrations + invoke_handler list), app/src-tauri/capabilities/default.json (existing permissions), app/src-tauri/tests/capabilities.rs (the test you must not break), app/package.json, app/src/App.tsx + main.tsx (where to mount UpdateChecker), Makefile (existing build-app target), .github/workflows/test.yml lines 22-60. This 5-minute read prevents the 80% of avoidable mistakes.\n\n2. **Keypair generation is a one-shot manual step in T3.** Run `pnpm -C app tauri signer generate -w ~/.tauri/dataclaw.key` outside the repo. Capture stdout: it will print `Public key:` followed by a base64 string. Paste THAT into tauri.conf.json `plugins.updater.pubkey`. Stash the private key file at `~/.tauri/dataclaw.key` and the [REDACTED] the maintainer (not git) can retrieve them \u2014 both will become GitHub Secrets `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` (file contents) and `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD`. If the secret is later rotated, the public key in tauri.conf.json must be updated and a transition release signed with both old + new keys (documented in RELEASING.md per T1).\n\n3. **Atomic compile group: T4 \u2192 T5 \u2192 T6.** Adding `tauri-plugin-updater` to Cargo.toml without registering the plugin compiles fine (Rust ignores unused deps), but registering the plugin without the dep won't. Add the dep first, then write updater.rs + main.rs edits, then capability. The compile won't pass cleanly until T4+T5+T6 are all done; sequence them in one branch.\n\n4. **The signingIdentity shell-substitution caveat.** `\"signingIdentity\": \"${APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY-}\"` relies on Tauri's bundler expanding the variable from process env at build time AND treating empty/null as \"skip codesign\". This is the Tauri 2 documented behavior, but verify with `make release-local` after T3 (validation step (4) of T14). If the substitution fails to expand or the empty string fails as a literal identity, the documented fallback is a Makefile-level JSON patch \u2014 note the deviation in executor_notes for T3 if you need it.\n\n5. **release.yml secret-name \u2192 env-var mapping is the most-flagged risk.** The GitHub Secret stays named `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` (Apple's terminology). The env block remaps it: `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` because Tauri's notarization code reads `APPLE_API_KEY`. The test `test_release_yml_maps_api_key_id_to_apple_api_key` should regex-match `APPLE_API_KEY:\\s*\\$\\{\\{\\s*secrets\\.APPLE_API_KEY_ID\\s*\\}\\}` (or equivalent) across lines, since YAML may break across two lines.\n\n6. **The `--target` flag is mandatory.** Without `tauri build --target aarch64-apple-darwin` (and the matching x86_64 invocation), GitHub Actions runner labels alone cannot guarantee architecture separation, and the matrix can ship two arm64 binaries with the same arch in `latest.json`.\n\n7. **Do not run actual `pnpm tauri build` on Linux/CI in T14.** Validation step (4) (`make release-local`) only works on macOS with Xcode toolchain. If executing in a non-macOS environment or without the Tauri toolchain, document the skip in T14 executor_notes with the specific reason (\"no macOS host available\"), and verify static config + run pytest + cargo test as a fallback. Do not attempt to fake the build success.\n\n8. **Avoid these debt-watch traps:** don't make tauri.conf.json `pubkey` an env substitution (debt: `tauri-config`); don't add new dependencies for tests that can be done with stdlib (debt avoidance pattern); don't break existing capabilities tests (debt: not regressing existing tests).\n\n9. **Repo slug for the updater endpoint URL** is `banodoco/dataclaw` per `pyproject.toml` Homepage/Repository. Verify before pasting into tauri.conf.json.\n\n10. **Manual `info` criteria (notarization spctl check, end-to-end updater install) cannot be automated in T14.** Surface them clearly in the PR description so the maintainer can run them after pushing v0.4.0-rc.1 to a test fork.",

The build step's `env:` block MUST include all of: `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` (the secret name keeps Apple terminology; Tauri reads the env var named APPLE_API_KEY), `APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY_PATH: <path>`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}`. All 9 documented secrets (including `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`) must appear somewhere in the file. Job `publish`: `needs: [build]`, runs on `ubuntu-latest`, downloads both arch artifacts, runs `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` to produce `latest.json`, then uses `gh release create $TAG --generate-notes` (or `gh release upload` if release exists) to publish the `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig`, and `latest.json`.",

Then create `app/src/components/UpdateChecker.tsx` (or matching convention) that imports `invoke` from `@tauri-apps/api/core`, renders a 'Check for updates' button, calls `invoke('check_for_updates')` on click, and on a non-null result shows version + release notes + an 'Install' button that calls `invoke('install_update')`. Mount it in the existing main/dashboard view inside an 'About' section (or place in a settings/about route if one already exists). Critical empty-key safety: if `check_for_updates` rejects with an error message containing 'pubkey' or 'signature', surface 'Updater not configured for this build' rather than throwing \u2014 this keeps unsigned local dev builds friendly even if a developer clicks the button.",

    {
      "id": "T9",
      "description": "Create `.github/workflows/release.yml` triggered on `push: tags: ['v*']` with two jobs. Job `build`: matrix uses explicit `include` entries \u2014 `{os: macos-14, target: aarch64-apple-darwin, arch: arm64}` and `{os: macos-13, target: x86_64-apple-darwin, arch: x86_64}`. Steps: checkout \u2192 setup-python 3.12 \u2192 setup-node 20 + pnpm 9 \u2192 rust toolchain (`dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable` with `targets: ${{ matrix.target }}`) \u2192 `pip install -e \".[dev,build]\"` \u2192 `make build-sidecar ARCH=${{ matrix.arch }}` \u2192 `pnpm -C app install --frozen-lockfile` \u2192 import .p12 cert into a temporary keychain (random password, deleted via `trap` on exit) using `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64` + `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD` \u2192 write .p8 to `~/.appstoreconnect/private_keys/AuthKey_${APPLE_API_KEY_ID}.p8` from `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64` \u2192 `pnpm -C app tauri build --target ${{ matrix.target }}` (the `--target` flag is mandatory) \u2192 upload `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig` artifacts named with `${{ matrix.target }}` for disambiguation. The build step's `env:` block MUST include all of: `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` (the secret name keeps Apple terminology; Tauri reads the env var named APPLE_API_KEY), `APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY_PATH: <path>`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}`. All 9 documented secrets (including `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`) must appear somewhere in the file. Job `publish`: `needs: [build]`, runs on `ubuntu-latest`, downloads both arch artifacts, runs `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` to produce `latest.json`, then uses `gh release create $TAG --generate-notes` (or `gh release upload` if release exists) to publish the `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig`, and `latest.json`.",
      "depends_on": [
        "T1",
        "T3"
      ],
      "status": "done",
      "executor_notes": "Created .github/workflows/release.yml triggered by v* tags with build and publish jobs. Build matrix uses explicit macos-14/aarch64-apple-darwin/arm64 and macos-13/x86_64-apple-darwin/x86_64 entries; build passes `--target ${{ matrix.target }}`. Workflow references all 9 documented secrets, maps `secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID` to env var `APPLE_API_KEY`, imports the Developer ID .p12 into a temporary keychain with cleanup trap, writes the .p8 to the App Store Connect key path, uploads target-disambiguated dmg/tarball/signature artifacts, and publishes assets plus latest.json via gh release create/upload. Validated with static assertions and Ruby YAML parsing. Full pytest passed.",

        ".github/workflows/release.yml"

      "id": "T10",
      "description": "Create `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` (executable, 0755) starting with `set -euo pipefail`. Inputs via env: `VERSION`, `RELEASE_TAG`, and paths to per-arch `.app.tar.gz` + `.sig` files (e.g. `ARM64_TARBALL`, `ARM64_SIG`, `X86_TARBALL`, `X86_SIG`). The script reads each `.sig` via `cat`, fails fast if either platform's `.sig` is missing (so we never publish a `latest.json` pointing at a nonexistent asset), and emits JSON conforming to tauri-plugin-updater v2's `latest.json` schema: top-level `version`, `pub_date` (ISO 8601 UTC), `notes` (optional), and `platforms` containing `darwin-aarch64` and `darwin-x86_64`, each with `signature` (.sig file contents) and `url` (https://github.com/banodoco/dataclaw/releases/download/${VERSION}/<asset>). Output to stdout or to `latest.json` depending on the calling convention used in release.yml. Make sure to chmod +x and that git tracks the executable bit.",
      "depends_on": [
        "T9"
      ],
      "status": "done",
      "executor_notes": "Created scripts/build-latest-json.sh with executable mode 0755 and set -euo pipefail. Validated bash syntax, executable mode, happy-path JSON contents, and missing-signature failure behavior. Full pytest passed.",

  "watch_items": [
    "FLAG-001 (addressed): tauri.conf.json `plugins.updater.pubkey` MUST hold the literal ed25519 public key emitted by `pnpm tauri signer generate` \u2014 never an empty string and never an env-substitution placeholder. Test `test_tauri_conf_has_updater_plugin` enforces non-empty.",
    "FLAG-002 (addressed): `bundle.createUpdaterArtifacts: true` is required so the Tauri build emits the `.app.tar.gz` + `.sig` pair the updater verifies against. Without this, release.yml uploads non-existent files.",
    "FLAG-003 (addressed): GitHub Secret name is `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` (matching Apple's terminology) but Tauri 2 reads the env var named `APPLE_API_KEY`. The release.yml env block MUST map them: `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}`.",
    "FLAG-004 (addressed): macos-14 vs macos-13 runner labels alone don't guarantee arm64 vs x86_64 builds. The release matrix MUST use explicit `include` entries pairing each OS to its `target` triple, AND `tauri build --target ${{ matrix.target }}` must be passed explicitly to prevent duplicate-arch artifacts.",
    "FLAG-005 (addressed): The Rust updater commands need a UI invocation path. The `UpdateChecker` React component must be mounted on a reachable view, and must gracefully handle 'pubkey'/'signature' errors so unsigned dev builds don't crash.",
    "Hard back-compat constraint: `make release-local` (= `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY=\"\" pnpm -C app tauri build`) MUST still produce an unsigned `.app` after T3. The `\"signingIdentity\": \"${APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY-}\"` shell-substitution in tauri.conf.json relies on Tauri's bundler treating empty as 'skip codesign'. If empirical testing disproves this, fall back to a Makefile-level JSON patch \u2014 note the deviation in executor_notes.",
    "The existing CI matrix at `.github/workflows/test.yml:26` `[macos-14, macos-13]` with `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: \"\"` (line 48-50) MUST stay green. T11 is the explicit checkpoint.",
    "Private-key / password leak risk: NEVER commit the ed25519 private key file or its password to the repo, screenshots, or transcripts. Only the public key goes into tauri.conf.json. The maintainer adds the private materials to GitHub Secrets out-of-band.",
    "All 9 GitHub Secrets must be referenced in release.yml: `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`, `APPLE_API_KEY_ID`, `APPLE_API_ISSUER`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID`, `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD`. Test `test_release_yml_has_required_secrets` enforces this.",
    "Entitlements default: `com.apple.security.cs.disable-library-validation = false`. Only flip to `true` if the bundled PyInstaller sidecar fails to load shared libs \u2014 and document the why with an inline comment.",
    "Repo slug assumption: updater endpoint URL is `https://github.com/banodoco/dataclaw/releases/latest/download/latest.json` (matches `pyproject.toml` Homepage/Repository). Confirm against pyproject.toml during T3.",
    "`build-latest-json.sh` MUST refuse to emit JSON if either platform's `.sig` file is missing or empty \u2014 preventing publication of a `latest.json` that points at nonexistent assets.",
    "Existing test `capabilities_file_grants_shell_sidecar_for_dataclaw` in `app/src-tauri/tests/capabilities.rs` must still pass \u2014 only ADD `updater:default`, do not remove or rename existing permissions.",
    "PyYAML is NOT a new dependency. Tests parse workflow YAML and tauri.conf.json with plain text + regex / json.load \u2014 keep it that way.",
    "Debt watch: 'tauri-config: updater public key planned as env substitution `${tauri_updater_pubkey}` rather than literal-committed' \u2014 actively avoid this anti-pattern in T3. Use the literal pubkey string.",

  "meta_commentary": "EXECUTION GUIDANCE:\n\n1. **Read the existing surface first.** Before T3, open and skim: app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json (current bundle/externalBin shape), app/src-tauri/Cargo.toml (existing plugin deps), app/src-tauri/src/main.rs (current plugin registrations + invoke_handler list), app/src-tauri/capabilities/default.json (existing permissions), app/src-tauri/tests/capabilities.rs (the test you must not break), app/package.json, app/src/App.tsx + main.tsx (where to mount UpdateChecker), Makefile (existing build-app target), .github/workflows/test.yml lines 22-60. This 5-minute read prevents the 80% of avoidable mistakes.\n\n2. **Keypair generation is a one-shot manual step in T3.** Run `pnpm -C app tauri signer generate -w ~/.tauri/dataclaw.key` outside the repo. Capture stdout: it will print `Public key:` followed by a base64 string. Paste THAT into tauri.conf.json `plugins.updater.pubkey`. Stash the private key file at `~/.tauri/dataclaw.key` and the [REDACTED] the maintainer (not git) can retrieve them \u2014 both will become GitHub Secrets `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` (file contents) and `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD`. If the secret is later rotated, the public key in tauri.conf.json must be updated and a transition release signed with both old + new keys (documented in RELEASING.md per T1).\n\n3. **Atomic compile group: T4 \u2192 T5 \u2192 T6.** Adding `tauri-plugin-updater` to Cargo.toml without registering the plugin compiles fine (Rust ignores unused deps), but registering the plugin without the dep won't. Add the dep first, then write updater.rs + main.rs edits, then capability. The compile won't pass cleanly until T4+T5+T6 are all done; sequence them in one branch.\n\n4. **The signingIdentity shell-substitution caveat.** `\"signingIdentity\": \"${APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY-}\"` relies on Tauri's bundler expanding the variable from process env at build time AND treating empty/null as \"skip codesign\". This is the Tauri 2 documented behavior, but verify with `make release-local` after T3 (validation step (4) of T14). If the substitution fails to expand or the empty string fails as a literal identity, the documented fallback is a Makefile-level JSON patch \u2014 note the deviation in executor_notes for T3 if you need it.\n\n5. **release.yml secret-name \u2192 env-var mapping is the most-flagged risk.** The GitHub Secret stays named `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` (Apple's terminology). The env block remaps it: `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` because Tauri's notarization code reads `APPLE_API_KEY`. The test `test_release_yml_maps_api_key_id_to_apple_api_key` should regex-match `APPLE_API_KEY:\\s*\\$\\{\\{\\s*secrets\\.APPLE_API_KEY_ID\\s*\\}\\}` (or equivalent) across lines, since YAML may break across two lines.\n\n6. **The `--target` flag is mandatory.** Without `tauri build --target aarch64-apple-darwin` (and the matching x86_64 invocation), GitHub Actions runner labels alone cannot guarantee architecture separation, and the matrix can ship two arm64 binaries with the same arch in `latest.json`.\n\n7. **Do not run actual `pnpm tauri build` on Linux/CI in T14.** Validation step (4) (`make release-local`) only works on macOS with Xcode toolchain. If executing in a non-macOS environment or without the Tauri toolchain, document the skip in T14 executor_notes with the specific reason (\"no macOS host available\"), and verify static config + run pytest + cargo test as a fallback. Do not attempt to fake the build success.\n\n8. **Avoid these debt-watch traps:** don't make tauri.conf.json `pubkey` an env substitution (debt: `tauri-config`); don't add new dependencies for tests that can be done with stdlib (debt avoidance pattern); don't break existing capabilities tests (debt: not regressing existing tests).\n\n9. **Repo slug for the updater endpoint URL** is `banodoco/dataclaw` per `pyproject.toml` Homepage/Repository. Verify before pasting into tauri.conf.json.\n\n10. **Manual `info` criteria (notarization spctl check, end-to-end updater install) cannot be automated in T14.** Surface them clearly in the PR description so the maintainer can run them after pushing v0.4.0-rc.1 to a test fork.",

Steps: checkout \u2192 setup-python 3.12 \u2192 setup-node 20 + pnpm 9 \u2192 rust toolchain (`dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable` with `targets: ${{ matrix.target }}`) \u2192 `pip install -e \".[dev,build]\"` \u2192 `make build-sidecar ARCH=${{ matrix.arch }}` \u2192 `pnpm -C app install --frozen-lockfile` \u2192 import .p12 cert into a temporary keychain (random password, deleted via `trap` on exit) using `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64` + `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD` \u2192 write .p8 to `~/.appstoreconnect/private_keys/AuthKey_${APPLE_API_KEY_ID}.p8` from `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64` \u2192 `pnpm -C app tauri build --target ${{ matrix.target }}` (the `--target` flag is mandatory) \u2192 upload `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig` artifacts named with `${{ matrix.target }}` for disambiguation. The build step's `env:` block MUST include all of: `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` (the secret name keeps Apple terminology; Tauri reads the env var named APPLE_API_KEY), `APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY_PATH: <path>`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}`. All 9 documented secrets (including `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`) must appear somewhere in the file. Job `publish`: `needs: [build]`, runs on `ubuntu-latest`, downloads both arch artifacts, runs `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` to produce `latest.json`, then uses `gh release create $TAG --generate-notes` (or `gh release upload` if release exists) to publish the `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig`, and `latest.json`.",

      "T3"

Workflow references all 9 documented secrets, maps `secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID` to env var `APPLE_API_KEY`, imports the Developer ID .p12 into a temporary keychain with cleanup trap, writes the .p8 to the App Store Connect key path, uploads target-disambiguated dmg/tarball/signature artifacts, and publishes assets plus latest.json via gh release create/upload. Validated with static assertions and Ruby YAML parsing. Full pytest passed.",

The script reads each `.sig` via `cat`, fails fast if either platform's `.sig` is missing (so we never publish a `latest.json` pointing at a nonexistent asset), and emits JSON conforming to tauri-plugin-updater v2's `latest.json` schema: top-level `version`, `pub_date` (ISO 8601 UTC), `notes` (optional), and `platforms` containing `darwin-aarch64` and `darwin-x86_64`, each with `signature` (.sig file contents) and `url` (https://github.com/banodoco/dataclaw/releases/download/${VERSION}/<asset>).       "id": "T9",
      "description": "Create `.github/workflows/release.yml` triggered on `push: tags: ['v*']` with two jobs. Job `build`: matrix uses explicit `include` entries \u2014 `{os: macos-14, target: aarch64-apple-darwin, arch: arm64}` and `{os: macos-13, target: x86_64-apple-darwin, arch: x86_64}`. Steps: checkout \u2192 setup-python 3.12 \u2192 setup-node 20 + pnpm 9 \u2192 rust toolchain (`dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable` with `targets: ${{ matrix.target }}`) \u2192 `pip install -e \".[dev,build]\"` \u2192 `make build-sidecar ARCH=${{ matrix.arch }}` \u2192 `pnpm -C app install --frozen-lockfile` \u2192 import .p12 cert into a temporary keychain (random password, deleted via `trap` on exit) using `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64` + `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD` \u2192 write .p8 to `~/.appstoreconnect/private_keys/AuthKey_${APPLE_API_KEY_ID}.p8` from `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64` \u2192 `pnpm -C app tauri build --target ${{ matrix.target }}` (the `--target` flag is mandatory) \u2192 upload `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig` artifacts named with `${{ matrix.target }}` for disambiguation. The build step's `env:` block MUST include all of: `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` (the secret name keeps Apple terminology; Tauri reads the env var named APPLE_API_KEY), `APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY_PATH: <path>`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}`. All 9 documented secrets (including `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`) must appear somewhere in the file. Job `publish`: `needs: [build]`, runs on `ubuntu-latest`, downloads both arch artifacts, runs `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` to produce `latest.json`, then uses `gh release create $TAG --generate-notes` (or `gh release upload` if release exists) to publish the `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig`, and `latest.json`.",
      "depends_on": [
        "T1",
        "T3"
      ],
      "status": "done",
      "executor_notes": "Created .github/workflows/release.yml triggered by v* tags with build and publish jobs. Build matrix uses explicit macos-14/aarch64-apple-darwin/arm64 and macos-13/x86_64-apple-darwin/x86_64 entries; build passes `--target ${{ matrix.target }}`. Workflow references all 9 documented secrets, maps `secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID` to env var `APPLE_API_KEY`, imports the Developer ID .p12 into a temporary keychain with cleanup trap, writes the .p8 to the App Store Connect key path, uploads target-disambiguated dmg/tarball/signature artifacts, and publishes assets plus latest.json via gh release create/upload. Validated with static assertions and Ruby YAML parsing. Full pytest passed.",

      "id": "T10",
      "description": "Create `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` (executable, 0755) starting with `set -euo pipefail`. Inputs via env: `VERSION`, `RELEASE_TAG`, and paths to per-arch `.app.tar.gz` + `.sig` files (e.g. `ARM64_TARBALL`, `ARM64_SIG`, `X86_TARBALL`, `X86_SIG`). The script reads each `.sig` via `cat`, fails fast if either platform's `.sig` is missing (so we never publish a `latest.json` pointing at a nonexistent asset), and emits JSON conforming to tauri-plugin-updater v2's `latest.json` schema: top-level `version`, `pub_date` (ISO 8601 UTC), `notes` (optional), and `platforms` containing `darwin-aarch64` and `darwin-x86_64`, each with `signature` (.sig file contents) and `url` (https://github.com/banodoco/dataclaw/releases/download/${VERSION}/<asset>). Output to stdout or to `latest.json` depending on the calling convention used in release.yml. Make sure to chmod +x and that git tracks the executable bit.",

        "T9"
      ],
      "status": "done",

  "watch_items": [
    "FLAG-001 (addressed): tauri.conf.json `plugins.updater.pubkey` MUST hold the literal ed25519 public key emitted by `pnpm tauri signer generate` \u2014 never an empty string and never an env-substitution placeholder. Test `test_tauri_conf_has_updater_plugin` enforces non-empty.",
    "FLAG-002 (addressed): `bundle.createUpdaterArtifacts: true` is required so the Tauri build emits the `.app.tar.gz` + `.sig` pair the updater verifies against. Without this, release.yml uploads non-existent files.",
    "FLAG-003 (addressed): GitHub Secret name is `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` (matching Apple's terminology) but Tauri 2 reads the env var named `APPLE_API_KEY`. The release.yml env block MUST map them: `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}`.",
    "FLAG-004 (addressed): macos-14 vs macos-13 runner labels alone don't guarantee arm64 vs x86_64 builds. The release matrix MUST use explicit `include` entries pairing each OS to its `target` triple, AND `tauri build --target ${{ matrix.target }}` must be passed explicitly to prevent duplicate-arch artifacts.",
    "FLAG-005 (addressed): The Rust updater commands need a UI invocation path. The `UpdateChecker` React component must be mounted on a reachable view, and must gracefully handle 'pubkey'/'signature' errors so unsigned dev builds don't crash.",
    "Hard back-compat constraint: `make release-local` (= `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY=\"\" pnpm -C app tauri build`) MUST still produce an unsigned `.app` after T3. The `\"signingIdentity\": \"${APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY-}\"` shell-substitution in tauri.conf.json relies on Tauri's bundler treating empty as 'skip codesign'. If empirical testing disproves this, fall back to a Makefile-level JSON patch \u2014 note the deviation in executor_notes.",
    "The existing CI matrix at `.github/workflows/test.yml:26` `[macos-14, macos-13]` with `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: \"\"` (line 48-50) MUST stay green. T11 is the explicit checkpoint.",
    "Private-key / password leak risk: NEVER commit the ed25519 private key file or its password to the repo, screenshots, or transcripts. Only the public key goes into tauri.conf.json. The maintainer adds the private materials to GitHub Secrets out-of-band.",
    "All 9 GitHub Secrets must be referenced in release.yml: `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`, `APPLE_API_KEY_ID`, `APPLE_API_ISSUER`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID`, `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD`. Test `test_release_yml_has_required_secrets` enforces this.",
    "Entitlements default: `com.apple.security.cs.disable-library-validation = false`. Only flip to `true` if the bundled PyInstaller sidecar fails to load shared libs \u2014 and document the why with an inline comment.",
    "Repo slug assumption: updater endpoint URL is `https://github.com/banodoco/dataclaw/releases/latest/download/latest.json` (matches `pyproject.toml` Homepage/Repository). Confirm against pyproject.toml during T3.",
    "`build-latest-json.sh` MUST refuse to emit JSON if either platform's `.sig` file is missing or empty \u2014 preventing publication of a `latest.json` that points at nonexistent assets.",
    "Existing test `capabilities_file_grants_shell_sidecar_for_dataclaw` in `app/src-tauri/tests/capabilities.rs` must still pass \u2014 only ADD `updater:default`, do not remove or rename existing permissions.",
    "PyYAML is NOT a new dependency. Tests parse workflow YAML and tauri.conf.json with plain text + regex / json.load \u2014 keep it that way.",
    "Debt watch: 'tauri-config: updater public key planned as env substitution `${tauri_updater_pubkey}` rather than literal-committed' \u2014 actively avoid this anti-pattern in T3. Use the literal pubkey string.",

  "meta_commentary": "EXECUTION GUIDANCE:\n\n1. **Read the existing surface first.** Before T3, open and skim: app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json (current bundle/externalBin shape), app/src-tauri/Cargo.toml (existing plugin deps), app/src-tauri/src/main.rs (current plugin registrations + invoke_handler list), app/src-tauri/capabilities/default.json (existing permissions), app/src-tauri/tests/capabilities.rs (the test you must not break), app/package.json, app/src/App.tsx + main.tsx (where to mount UpdateChecker), Makefile (existing build-app target), .github/workflows/test.yml lines 22-60. This 5-minute read prevents the 80% of avoidable mistakes.\n\n2. **Keypair generation is a one-shot manual step in T3.** Run `pnpm -C app tauri signer generate -w ~/.tauri/dataclaw.key` outside the repo. Capture stdout: it will print `Public key:` followed by a base64 string. Paste THAT into tauri.conf.json `plugins.updater.pubkey`. Stash the private key file at `~/.tauri/dataclaw.key` and the [REDACTED] the maintainer (not git) can retrieve them \u2014 both will become GitHub Secrets `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` (file contents) and `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD`. If the secret is later rotated, the public key in tauri.conf.json must be updated and a transition release signed with both old + new keys (documented in RELEASING.md per T1).\n\n3. **Atomic compile group: T4 \u2192 T5 \u2192 T6.** Adding `tauri-plugin-updater` to Cargo.toml without registering the plugin compiles fine (Rust ignores unused deps), but registering the plugin without the dep won't. Add the dep first, then write updater.rs + main.rs edits, then capability. The compile won't pass cleanly until T4+T5+T6 are all done; sequence them in one branch.\n\n4. **The signingIdentity shell-substitution caveat.** `\"signingIdentity\": \"${APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY-}\"` relies on Tauri's bundler expanding the variable from process env at build time AND treating empty/null as \"skip codesign\". This is the Tauri 2 documented behavior, but verify with `make release-local` after T3 (validation step (4) of T14). If the substitution fails to expand or the empty string fails as a literal identity, the documented fallback is a Makefile-level JSON patch \u2014 note the deviation in executor_notes for T3 if you need it.\n\n5. **release.yml secret-name \u2192 env-var mapping is the most-flagged risk.** The GitHub Secret stays named `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` (Apple's terminology). The env block remaps it: `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` because Tauri's notarization code reads `APPLE_API_KEY`. The test `test_release_yml_maps_api_key_id_to_apple_api_key` should regex-match `APPLE_API_KEY:\\s*\\$\\{\\{\\s*secrets\\.APPLE_API_KEY_ID\\s*\\}\\}` (or equivalent) across lines, since YAML may break across two lines.\n\n6. **The `--target` flag is mandatory.** Without `tauri build --target aarch64-apple-darwin` (and the matching x86_64 invocation), GitHub Actions runner labels alone cannot guarantee architecture separation, and the matrix can ship two arm64 binaries with the same arch in `latest.json`.\n\n7. **Do not run actual `pnpm tauri build` on Linux/CI in T14.** Validation step (4) (`make release-local`) only works on macOS with Xcode toolchain. If executing in a non-macOS environment or without the Tauri toolchain, document the skip in T14 executor_notes with the specific reason (\"no macOS host available\"), and verify static config + run pytest + cargo test as a fallback. Do not attempt to fake the build success.\n\n8. **Avoid these debt-watch traps:** don't make tauri.conf.json `pubkey` an env substitution (debt: `tauri-config`); don't add new dependencies for tests that can be done with stdlib (debt avoidance pattern); don't break existing capabilities tests (debt: not regressing existing tests).\n\n9. **Repo slug for the updater endpoint URL** is `banodoco/dataclaw` per `pyproject.toml` Homepage/Repository. Verify before pasting into tauri.conf.json.\n\n10. **Manual `info` criteria (notarization spctl check, end-to-end updater install) cannot be automated in T14.** Surface them clearly in the PR description so the maintainer can run them after pushing v0.4.0-rc.1 to a test fork.",

Job `build`: matrix uses explicit `include` entries \u2014 `{os: macos-14, target: aarch64-apple-darwin, arch: arm64}` and `{os: macos-13, target: x86_64-apple-darwin, arch: x86_64}`. Steps: checkout \u2192 setup-python 3.12 \u2192 setup-node 20 + pnpm 9 \u2192 rust toolchain (`dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable` with `targets: ${{ matrix.target }}`) \u2192 `pip install -e \".[dev,build]\"` \u2192 `make build-sidecar ARCH=${{ matrix.arch }}` \u2192 `pnpm -C app install --frozen-lockfile` \u2192 import .p12 cert into a temporary keychain (random password, deleted via `trap` on exit) using `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64` + `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD` \u2192 write .p8 to `~/.appstoreconnect/private_keys/AuthKey_${APPLE_API_KEY_ID}.p8` from `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64` \u2192 `pnpm -C app tauri build --target ${{ matrix.target }}` (the `--target` flag is mandatory) \u2192 upload `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig` artifacts named with `${{ matrix.target }}` for disambiguation. The build step's `env:` block MUST include all of: `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` (the secret name keeps Apple terminology; Tauri reads the env var named APPLE_API_KEY), `APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY_PATH: <path>`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}`. All 9 documented secrets (including `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`) must appear somewhere in the file. Job `publish`: `needs: [build]`, runs on `ubuntu-latest`, downloads both arch artifacts, runs `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` to produce `latest.json`, then uses `gh release create $TAG --generate-notes` (or `gh release upload` if release exists) to publish the `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig`, and `latest.json`.",

Workflow references all 9 documented secrets, maps `secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID` to env var `APPLE_API_KEY`, imports the Developer ID .p12 into a temporary keychain with cleanup trap, writes the .p8 to the App Store Connect key path, uploads target-disambiguated dmg/tarball/signature artifacts, and publishes assets plus latest.json via gh release create/upload.       "id": "T9",
      "description": "Create `.github/workflows/release.yml` triggered on `push: tags: ['v*']` with two jobs. Job `build`: matrix uses explicit `include` entries \u2014 `{os: macos-14, target: aarch64-apple-darwin, arch: arm64}` and `{os: macos-13, target: x86_64-apple-darwin, arch: x86_64}`. Steps: checkout \u2192 setup-python 3.12 \u2192 setup-node 20 + pnpm 9 \u2192 rust toolchain (`dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable` with `targets: ${{ matrix.target }}`) \u2192 `pip install -e \".[dev,build]\"` \u2192 `make build-sidecar ARCH=${{ matrix.arch }}` \u2192 `pnpm -C app install --frozen-lockfile` \u2192 import .p12 cert into a temporary keychain (random password, deleted via `trap` on exit) using `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64` + `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD` \u2192 write .p8 to `~/.appstoreconnect/private_keys/AuthKey_${APPLE_API_KEY_ID}.p8` from `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64` \u2192 `pnpm -C app tauri build --target ${{ matrix.target }}` (the `--target` flag is mandatory) \u2192 upload `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig` artifacts named with `${{ matrix.target }}` for disambiguation. The build step's `env:` block MUST include all of: `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}` (the secret name keeps Apple terminology; Tauri reads the env var named APPLE_API_KEY), `APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}`, `APPLE_API_KEY_PATH: <path>`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}`, `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}`. All 9 documented secrets (including `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`) must appear somewhere in the file. Job `publish`: `needs: [build]`, runs on `ubuntu-latest`, downloads both arch artifacts, runs `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` to produce `latest.json`, then uses `gh release create $TAG --generate-notes` (or `gh release upload` if release exists) to publish the `.dmg`, `.app.tar.gz`, `.app.tar.gz.sig`, and `latest.json`.",

        "T1",
        "T3"
      ],
      "status": "done",
      "executor_notes": "Created .github/workflows/release.yml triggered by v* tags with build and publish jobs. Build matrix uses explicit macos-14/aarch64-apple-darwin/arm64 and macos-13/x86_64-apple-darwin/x86_64 entries; build passes `--target ${{ matrix.target }}`. Workflow references all 9 documented secrets, maps `secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID` to env var `APPLE_API_KEY`, imports the Developer ID .p12 into a temporary keychain with cleanup trap, writes the .p8 to the App Store Connect key path, uploads target-disambiguated dmg/tarball/signature artifacts, and publishes assets plus latest.json via gh release create/upload. Validated with static assertions and Ruby YAML parsing. Full pytest passed.",

      "description": "Create `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` (executable, 0755) starting with `set -euo pipefail`. The script reads each `.sig` via `cat`, fails fast if either platform's `.sig` is missing (so we never publish a `latest.json` pointing at a nonexistent asset), and emits JSON conforming to tauri-plugin-updater v2's `latest.json` schema: top-level `version`, `pub_date` (ISO 8601 UTC), `notes` (optional), and `platforms` containing `darwin-aarch64` and `darwin-x86_64`, each with `signature` (.sig file contents) and `url` (https://github.com/banodoco/dataclaw/releases/download/${VERSION}/<asset>). Output to stdout or to `latest.json` depending on the calling convention used in release.yml. Make sure to chmod +x and that git tracks the executable bit.",
